List of PlayStation 5 games
{{Short description|none}}
{{Use mdy dates|date=July 2021}}
This is a list of games for the [[PlayStation 5]]. Physical games are sold on [[Ultra HD Blu-ray]] and digital games can be purchased through the [[PlayStation Store]]. The PlayStation 5 is [[backwards compatible]] with all but six [[List of PlayStation 4 games|PlayStation 4 games]].<ref>{{Cite web |title=Backward compatibility: PS4 games playable on PS5 consoles |url=https://www.playstation.com/en-us/support/games/ps5-backward-compatibility-games/ |access-date=2026-08-17 |website=www.playstation.com}}</ref> This list only includes games that are released natively for PlayStation 5. [[PlayStation VR2]] and backwards compatible games are excluded.<ref>{{cite web|url=https://www.pushsquare.com/guides/ps4-to-ps5-all-games-with-confirmed-free-upgrades|title=PS4 to PS5: All Games with Confirmed Free Upgrades|website=Push Square|access-date=November 20, 2020|archive-date=June 10, 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220610112351/https://www.pushsquare.com/guides/ps4-to-ps5-all-games-with-confirmed-free-upgrades|url-status=live}}</ref>

There are currently '''{{#expr:{{table row counter|id=softwarelist|ignore=3}}}}''' games on this list.{{efn|This number is always up to date by this [[Template:Table row counter|script]].}}
